{"forms":[{"form":"flutters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The eagle soared across the horizon with just a few slow flutters of its wings. The flutter of insects and bats is more rapid. Some birds also use non-flight flutters as communication and in thermoregulation.","type":"example"},{"text":"Fish move using a flutter of their body to propel themselves through the water. Some also derive propulsion or maneuverability from their fins' flutter.","type":"example"},{"text":"Some turtles use their claw flutter as a means of communication, particularly in mating.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of fluttering, or multiple acts thereof; the back-and-forth motion of fluttering; a state of fluttering."],"synonyms":[{"word":"flap"},{"word":"fluttering"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"leaf flutter; feather flutter","type":"example"},{"text":"The flutter of the flock buffeted me.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of fluttering, or multiple acts thereof; the back-and-forth motion of fluttering; a state of fluttering.","A flapping, crackling or rustling sound, as from a fluttering motion or any comparable sound; any series of quick repetitive sounds."],"synonyms":[{"word":"flap"},{"word":"fluttering"}],"tags":["broadly","countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A flutter of gasps spread over the crowd.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of fluttering, or multiple acts thereof; the back-and-forth motion of fluttering; a state of fluttering.","A flapping, crackling or rustling sound, as from a fluttering motion or any comparable sound; any series of quick repetitive sounds.","A sound that spreads in the manner of a wave or ripple."],"synonyms":[{"word":"flap"},{"word":"fluttering"}],"tags":["broadly","countable","figuratively","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He couldn't help but feel more attracted to her with each flutter of her eyes/lashes/eyelids.","type":"example"},{"text":"Her eyes did a flutter.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of fluttering, or multiple acts thereof; the back-and-forth motion of fluttering; a state of fluttering.","The rapid up-and-down movement of the eyelids, and consequently eyelashes, often seen as intentionally flirtatious or alluring and often attributed to the eyes or eyebrows."],"synonyms":[{"word":"flap"},{"word":"fluttering"},{"word":"bat"},{"word":"batting"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The flutter of the geisha's fan was elegant.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of fluttering, or multiple acts thereof;
